**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/indoors/generate-facility-entryways.html

**id**　94afa816c49e5aff1a-schema

**kind**　版本參數表

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/indoors/generate-facility-entryways.html#parameters

**source_language**　en

**content_version**　3.8

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/tool-reference/indoors/generate-facility-entryways.html#parameters

**parameters**　[{"parameter": "in_level_features", "label_zh": "輸入樓層圖徵", "label_en": "Input Level Features", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Feature Layer", "description_en": "The input polygon features representing a level or levels in one or more facilities. In the Indoors model, this is the Levels layer. Only the levels represented by these features will be processed."}, {"parameter": "in_unit_features", "label_zh": "輸入單元圖徵", "label_en": "Input Unit Features", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Feature Layer", "description_en": "The input polygon features representing building spaces. In the Indoors model, this is the Units layer. The tool will use these features when identifying exterior edges of a facility."}, {"parameter": "in_door_features", "label_zh": "輸入門圖徵", "label_en": "Input Door Features", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Feature Layer", "description_en": "The input polyline features representing doors. In the Indoors model, this is a subset of features from the Details layer. The tool will use these features when identifying entryways along the exterior of a facility. Note: The layer must have one or more door features selected for the tool to run. Use the Select Layer By Attribute tool to make a selection."}, {"parameter": "target_entryways", "label_zh": "目標入口", "label_en": "Target Entryways", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Feature Layer", "description_en": "The feature class or feature layer to which generated entryway points will be written."}, {"parameter": "buffer_size", "label_zh": "緩衝區大小 （可選）", "label_en": "Buffer Size (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Double", "description_en": "The distance, in meters, the tool will search inward and outward from a facility's exterior edge to identify potential entryways. The value must be greater than 0 and less than 10. The default is 0.5."}, {"parameter": "entryway_use_type", "label_zh": "入口使用類型 （可選）", "label_en": "Entryway Use Type (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "The value that will be used to calculate the USE_TYPE field for new entryway points. The default is Entry."}, {"parameter": "exterior_unit_exp", "label_zh": "外部單位表達式 （可選）", "label_en": "Exterior Unit Expression (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "SQL Expression", "description_en": "An SQL expression that will be used to define which values from the Input Unit Features parameter value represent a facility's exterior spaces, such as patios or fire escapes. Spaces matching this expression will be treated as exterior features during entryway generation."}, {"parameter": "delete_existing_entryways", "label_zh": "刪除現有入口 （可選）", "label_en": "Delete Existing Entryways (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Boolean", "description_en": "Specifies whether existing entryway features with a USE_TYPE field value matching the Entryway Use Type parameter value will be deleted before creating new entryway points. When deleting existing entryways, the tool only identifies entryways on levels included in the Input Level Features parameter value. Checked—Existing features will be deleted. Unchecked—Existing features will not be deleted. This is the default."}, {"parameter": "level_id_field", "label_zh": "樓層 ID 欄位 （可選）", "label_en": "Level ID Field (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Field", "description_en": "The field that will be updated with the associated level ID for the new entryway features. If the Input Level Features parameter value is a floor-aware layer, this parameter will default to the layer's configured Floor Field value. Otherwise, the parameter will default to the LEVEL_ID field. If the defined field does not exist in the Target Entryways parameter value, a new field with the supplied name will be created and populated with the level ID field values."}, {"parameter": "use_type_field", "label_zh": "使用類型欄位 （可選）", "label_en": "Use Type Field (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Field", "description_en": "The field that will be updated with the Entryway Use Type value for the new entryway features. The default is the USE_TYPE field. If the defined field does not exist in the Target Entryways parameter value, a field with the supplied name will be created and populated with the Entryway Use Type value."}]

**signatures**　
- arcpy.indoors.GenerateFacilityEntryways(in_level_features, in_unit_features, in_door_features, target_entryways, {buffer_size}, {entryway_use_type}, {exterior_unit_exp}, {delete_existing_entryways}, {level_id_field}, {use_type_field})

**text**　依英文版參數表順序列出：in_level_features、in_unit_features、in_door_features、target_entryways、buffer_size、entryway_use_type、exterior_unit_exp、delete_existing_entryways、level_id_field、use_type_field。選填標示、資料型別與英文原說明見 parameters。

## 壓縮 (資料管理工具) · 用法

**id**　94c73ee42d20b506c3-2

**english**　Compress

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 資料管理工具箱
- 地理資料庫管理工具集

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/compress.html#usage

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/compress.html

**text**　要提高地理資料庫的性能，請定期壓縮地理資料庫。 壓縮后的地理資料庫效率更高。
地理資料庫壓縮后，將無法恢復刪除的記錄。
壓縮地理資料庫不僅可以降低空間需求，還可以縮短整體檢索時間。
盡管壓縮工具正在運行，您仍然可以查看地理資料庫中的資料。
只有地理資料庫管理員才能執行壓縮。
此工具不適用于不使用版本化的企業級地理資料庫。
此工具不適用于分支版本化。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/data-management/compress.html#usage

**steps**　
1. 開啟 Analysis > Tools，以英文名稱搜尋 Compress，核對工具箱及工具摘要。
2. 依工具窗格順序核對下列參數；展開同頁的參數卡可查說明、選項與資料型別。輸入資料庫連接 [in_workspace]
3. 依用法、環境與授權段落核對限制。確認輸出位置或就地修改範圍後執行 Run。
4. 在 Geoprocessing History 檢查執行狀態與訊息，再核對結果是否符合使用者指定的範圍與條件。

## Update Aviation Annotation (Aviation Tools) · 匯總

**id**　94cf74eba38b9710ea-1

**english**　Update Aviation Annotation

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- Aviation 工具箱
- 圖表工具集
- 制圖工具集

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/aviation/update-aviation-annotation.html#summary

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/aviation/update-aviation-annotation.html

**text**　Batch updates Aviation-specific annotation layers to streamline chart production and finishing.
This tool reapplies the label expressions for each annotation class in the input annotation layers. This will propagate any changes made to the label expressions to each annotation feature without having to regenerate all new annotation features. This is useful to implement any specification changes to chart annotation, as well as while developing and testing new annotation label expressions. You can also use this tool to apply scaling to annotation features to output them in a uniform size on charts of varying scales.
A geodatabase annotation feature class is stored with a defined, single reference scale. Annotation contains a FontSize field defining its size. Aviation annotation may also have its TextString field populated with text formatting tags containing font size values, which override the annotation's FontSize field value. These font size values reflect the font size of the annotation only when rendered in a map viewed at the reference scale of the annotation feature class. However, the annotation feature class may need to appear in a uniform font size in page units on charts produced at varying reference scales.

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/aviation/update-aviation-annotation.html#summary

## Update Aviation Annotation (Aviation Tools) · 注:

**id**　94cf74eba38b9710ea-2

**english**　Update Aviation Annotation

**text**　When the output chart scale does not match the reference scale of the annotation feature class, the annotation will render at an unintended size. In this case, the font sizes of the individual annotation features must be scaled to compensate for the difference.

## Update Aviation Annotation (Aviation Tools) · 用法

**id**　94cf74eba38b9710ea-3

**english**　Update Aviation Annotation

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- Aviation 工具箱
- 圖表工具集
- 制圖工具集

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/aviation/update-aviation-annotation.html#usage

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/aviation/update-aviation-annotation.html

**text**　The Annotation Layers parameter value must include feature-linked annotation.
On the tool dialog box, the SubLayers parameter will show selection check boxes for each annotation class in the annotation layer. 注: If an annotation class is not selected, it will be skipped.
This tool's Apply Scaling parameter will update the values of the FontSize field and any font size values within text formatting tags in the TextString field of the input annotation layer based on the reference scale of the input Area of Interest Features parameter value for the chart.
The formula used for scaling is: updated FontSize = ( LinkedCartoFeature.ReferenceScale * AnnotationLayer FontSize ) / AnnotationLayer ReferenceScale. The values used in the formula are explained below: LinkedCartoFeature.ReferenceScale is the ReferenceScale field value of the annotation feature's linked cartographic feature. LinkedCartoFeature.ReferenceScale is populated by the Generate Aviation Cartographic Features tool from the AIS_AOI.ReferenceClass field value. AnnotationLayer FontSize refers to both the FontSize field value and any font sizes within text formatting tags in the TextString field of the annotation features. These are the values that are updated in the updated FontSize part of the formula. AnnotationLayer ReferenceScale is the reference scale property of the source annotation feature class.

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/aviation/update-aviation-annotation.html#usage

## Update Aviation Annotation (Aviation Tools) · 用法

**id**　94cf74eba38b9710ea-4

**english**　Update Aviation Annotation

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- Aviation 工具箱
- 圖表工具集
- 制圖工具集

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/aviation/update-aviation-annotation.html#usage

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/aviation/update-aviation-annotation.html

**text**　The Area of Interest Features parameter is used to set a boundary within which airspace annotation features will be processed. Airspace polygon features that wholly or partially overlap the area of interest (AOI) will be processed. Use the AIS_AOI feature class from the AIS Charting data model as input for the Area of Interest Features parameter. 注: The tool will honor a definition query or selection on the Area of Interest Features parameter value. Otherwise, the tool will run using all features in the Area of Interest Features parameter value. 注意： This tool modifies the input data. See Tools that modify or update the input data for more information and strategies to avoid undesired data changes.

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/aviation/update-aviation-annotation.html#usage

## Update Aviation Annotation (Aviation Tools) · 代碼示例

**id**　94cf74eba38b9710ea-18

**english**　Update Aviation Annotation

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- Aviation 工具箱
- 圖表工具集
- 制圖工具集

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/aviation/update-aviation-annotation.html#derived-output

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/aviation/update-aviation-annotation.html

**text**　# Name: UpdateAviationAnno_sample.py
# Description: ArcGIS processing tool that automates
# the resizing of annotation layers.

# Import system modules
import arcpy

# Set the environment
arcpy.env.workspace = r"D:\Data\AIS.gdb\Data"

# Set local variables
in_map = "Map"
in_anno_layers = "ADHP_C_A 'ADHP ENR;ADHP VIS';ATSRoute_C_A"
aoi_features = "AOI"
apply_scaling = "SCALED"

# Call the UpdateAviationAnno tool
arcpy.aviation.UpdateAviationAnno(in_map, in_anno_layers, aoi_features, apply_scaling)

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/aviation/update-aviation-annotation.html#derived-output

## Update Aviation Annotation (Aviation Tools) · 工具執行與核對

**chapter**

**kind**　工具操作

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/aviation/update-aviation-annotation.html#parameters

**steps**　
1. 開啟 Analysis > Tools，以英文名稱搜尋 Update Aviation Annotation，核對工具箱及工具摘要。
2. 依工具窗格順序核對下列參數；展開同頁的參數卡可查說明、選項與資料型別。Map [in_map]、Annotation Layers [in_anno_layers]、Area of Interest Features （可選） [aoi_features]、Apply scaling （可選） [apply_scaling]
3. 依用法、環境與授權段落核對限制。確認輸出位置或就地修改範圍後執行 Run。
4. 在 Geoprocessing History 檢查執行狀態與訊息，再核對結果是否符合使用者指定的範圍與條件。

**provenance**　依官方參數表編排的操作清單

**text**　執行圖徵的 80/20 分析，并根據關聯事件點的數量創建點聚類、線或面。 此工具將計算一個累積的百分比欄位，用于標識事件不成比例發生的位置。

**content_version**　3.7

## 80-20 分析 (犯罪分析和安全工具) · 用法

**id**　94f4cac13607ea8d89-2

**english**　80-20 Analysis

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 犯罪分析和安全工具箱

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/crime-analysis/eighty-twenty-analysis.html#usage

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/crime-analysis/eighty-twenty-analysis.html

**text**　80-20 規則是一個理論概念，其中大部分事件發生在少數位置，例如 80% 的事件發生在 20% 的位置。
在犯罪分析學科中，可以通過多種方式使用該工具。 典型的分析工作流包括以下內容： Aggregate incidents into clusters—此類型分析可識別特定時期內事件數量最多的屬性。 Aggregate incidents to street segments—此類型分析有時被描述為尋找熱點街道。 Aggregate incidents to specific neighborhood boundaries—此類型分析有時被描述為尋找熱點區域。
聚合方法參數，用于指定如何進行 80-20 分析以及如何聚合輸入點圖徵。 可用于進行分析的兩種聚合方法包括聚類和最近圖徵，其描述如下： 聚類—輸入點圖徵將根據基于密度的聚類 工具中使用的定義距離 (DBSCAN) 聚類方法進行聚類。 最近圖徵—輸入點圖徵將與最近的輸入比較線或面圖徵相關聯。
當將聚合方法參數設置為聚類時，以下欄位將添加到輸出中： ICOUNT—在該聚類的聚類容差內找到的點數。 PERC—在該聚類的聚類容差內找到的總點數的百分比。 CUMU_PERC—當前聚類點和所有其他較大聚類點的累積百分比（使用 ICOUNT 值計算）。 CUMU_LPERC—當前聚類點和所有其他較大聚類點的累積百分比（使用輸出點圖徵總數計算）。 CUMU_PERC 和 CUMU_LPERC 值可用于確定數量不成比例的聚類位置是否代表較大比例的犯罪，例如，20% 的聚類位置包含 80% 的總點數。 當將聚合方法參數設置為最近圖徵時，以下欄位將添加到輸出中： ICOUNT—發現的最接近線或面圖徵的點數。 PERC—在線或面圖徵附近或內部發現的點總數的百分比。 CUMU_PERC—當前圖徵和所有其他具有更大計數的圖徵的累積百分比（使用 ICOUNT 值計算）。 CUMU_LPERC—當前圖徵和所有其他具有更大計數的圖徵的累積百分比（使用輸出線或面圖徵總數計算）。 INC_KM—每千米的圖徵數。 當輸入比較圖徵值為線時，其將添加到輸出中。 INC_MI—每英里的圖徵數。 當輸入比較圖徵值為線時，其將添加到輸出中。 INC_SQKM—每平方千米的圖徵數。 當輸入比較圖徵值為面時，其將添加到輸出中。 INC_SQMI—每平方英里的圖徵數。 當輸入比較圖徵值為面時，其將添加到輸出中。 CUMU_PERC 和 CUMU_LPERC 值可用于確定數量不成比例的線或面圖徵是否代表較大比例的犯罪，例如，20% 的線或面圖徵位置包含 80% 的總點數。 將根據生成的 ICOUNT（事件計數）、CUMU_PERC（累積百分比）、PERC（事件百分比）和 CUMU_LPERC（累積位置百分比）欄位值對輸出記錄進行排序。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/crime-analysis/eighty-twenty-analysis.html#usage

使用基于每個位置發生的事件數量的分級符號圖層來對輸出點圖徵類進行符號化。

## 80-20 分析 (犯罪分析和安全工具) · 代碼示例

**id**　94f4cac13607ea8d89-23

**english**　80-20 Analysis

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 犯罪分析和安全工具箱

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/crime-analysis/eighty-twenty-analysis.html#parameters

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/crime-analysis/eighty-twenty-analysis.html

**text**　# Name: EightyTwentyAnalysis.py
# Description: Conducts an 80/20 analysis of 911 calls to determine clusters of calls within 50 meters of each other.

# import system modules
import arcpy

# Set environment settings
arcpy.env.workspace = r"C:\data\city_pd.gdb"

# Set local variables
in_features = "CallsForService"
out_feature_class = "80_20_clusters"
cluster_tolerance = "50 Meters"
out_fields = ["FULLADDR","RESCITY", "RESSTATE", "RESZIP5"]

# Run EightyTwentyAnalysis
arcpy.ca.EightyTwentyAnalysis(in_features,
                              out_feature_class,
                              cluster_tolerance,
                              out_fields)

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/crime-analysis/eighty-twenty-analysis.html#parameters

## 80-20 分析 (犯罪分析和安全工具) · 代碼示例

**id**　94f4cac13607ea8d89-25

**english**　80-20 Analysis

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 犯罪分析和安全工具箱

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/crime-analysis/eighty-twenty-analysis.html#parameters

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/crime-analysis/eighty-twenty-analysis.html

**text**　# Name: EightyTwentyAnalysisStreets.py
# Description: Conduct an 80/20 analysis of calls for service to determine street segments with a disproportional amount of crimes nearby.

# import system modules
import arcpy

# Set environment settings
arcpy.env.workspace = r"C:\data\city_pd.gdb"

# Set local variables
in_features = "CallsForService"
out_feature_class = "80_20_streets"
comp_features = "city_centerlines"
out_fields = ["STREET_NAME", "L_ADDR_NUM", "R_ADDR_NUM"]

# Run Eighty Twenty Analysis
arpcy.ca.EightyTwentyAnalysis(in_features,
                              out_feature_class,
                              in_comparison_features=comp_features,
                              out_fields=out_fields)

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/crime-analysis/eighty-twenty-analysis.html#parameters

## 80-20 分析 (犯罪分析和安全工具) · 工具執行與核對

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 犯罪分析和安全工具箱

**english**　80-20 Analysis

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/crime-analysis/eighty-twenty-analysis.html

**id**　94f4cac13607ea8d89-workflow

**kind**　工具操作

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/crime-analysis/eighty-twenty-analysis.html#parameters

**steps**　
1. 開啟 Analysis > Tools，以英文名稱搜尋 80-20 Analysis，核對工具箱及工具摘要。
2. 依工具窗格順序核對下列參數；展開同頁的參數卡可查說明、選項與資料型別。輸入點圖徵 [in_features]、輸出圖徵類 [out_feature_class]、拓撲容差 （可選） [cluster_tolerance]、輸出欄位 （可選） [out_fields]、聚合方法 （可選） [aggregation_method]、輸入比較圖徵 （可選） [in_comparison_features]、匹配欄位 （可選） [match_fields]
3. 依用法、環境與授權段落核對限制。確認輸出位置或就地修改範圍後執行 Run。
4. 在 Geoprocessing History 檢查執行狀態與訊息，再核對結果是否符合使用者指定的範圍與條件。

**provenance**　依官方參數表編排的操作清單

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/crime-analysis/eighty-twenty-analysis.html#parameters

## 80-20 分析 (犯罪分析和安全工具) · 英文版工具簽章與參數順序

**english**　80-20 Analysis

**id**　94f4cac13607ea8d89-schema

**kind**　版本參數表

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/crime-analysis/eighty-twenty-analysis.html#parameters

**source_language**　en

**content_version**　3.8

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/tool-reference/crime-analysis/eighty-twenty-analysis.html#parameters

**parameters**　[{"parameter": "in_features", "label_zh": "輸入點圖徵", "label_en": "Input Point Features", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Feature Layer", "description_en": "The input point features that will be used to create clusters, lines, or polygons."}, {"parameter": "out_feature_class", "label_zh": "輸出圖徵類", "label_en": "Output Feature Class", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Feature Class", "description_en": "The output feature class. When the Aggregation Method parameter is set to Cluster, the output will be a point feature class. When the Aggregation Method parameter is set to Closest Feature, the geometry type of the output will be the same as the Input Comparison Features parameter value."}, {"parameter": "cluster_tolerance", "label_zh": "拓撲容差 （可選）", "label_en": "Cluster Tolerance (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Linear Unit", "description_en": "The maximum distance separating points at which they will be considered part of the same cluster. If no cluster tolerance is specified, the tool will create a cluster where point features overlap. This parameter is active when the Aggregation Method parameter is set to Cluster."}, {"parameter": "out_fields", "label_zh": "輸出欄位 （可選）", "label_en": "Output Fields (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Field", "description_en": "The fields from the input features that will be transferred to the output."}, {"parameter": "aggregation_method", "label_zh": "聚合方法 （可選）", "label_en": "Aggregation Method (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Specifies how the input point features will be aggregated. Cluster—The input point features will be clustered. This is the default. Closest Feature—The input point features will be aggregated to the closest comparison polygon or line feature."}, {"parameter": "in_comparison_features", "label_zh": "輸入比較圖徵 （可選）", "label_en": "Input Comparison Features (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Feature Layer", "description_en": "The comparison input polygon or line feature class by which the Input Point Features parameter value is aggregated. This parameter is active when the Aggregation Method parameter is set to Closest Feature."}, {"parameter": "match_fields", "label_zh": "匹配欄位 （可選）", "label_en": "Match Fields (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Value Table", "description_en": "Pairs of fields from the input point features and input comparison features that will be used for attribute matching. Only the records from the input point features that share match field values with the input comparison features will participate in the spatial join. This parameter is active when the Aggregation Method parameter is set to Closest Feature. Value table columns: Point Fields—Fields from the input point features that will be used for attribute matching. Comparison Fields—Fields from the input comparison features that will be used for attribute matching."}]

**signatures**　
- arcpy.ca.EightyTwentyAnalysis(in_features, out_feature_class, {cluster_tolerance}, {out_fields}, {aggregation_method}, {in_comparison_features}, {match_fields})

**text**　依英文版參數表順序列出：in_features、out_feature_class、cluster_tolerance、out_fields、aggregation_method、in_comparison_features、match_fields。選填標示、資料型別與英文原說明見 parameters。

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/160001-170000/tool-errors-and-warnings-160676-160700-160693.html#solution

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/160001-170000/tool-errors-and-warnings-160676-160700-160693.html

**text**　雖然此錯誤可能會發生，但發生的頻率非常低，以至于我們尚且無法確定典型原因，也因此目前沒有可用的解決方案。
如果您能夠使用反饋表單來提供有關導致此錯誤的所用步驟的詳細信息，那么此舉將有助于我們在未來確定和提供解決方案。
幫助信息包括下列內容：
消息 ID 以及錯誤或警告消息
工具的名稱
用于參數的值
相關資料集詳細信息
如果該工具不再處于打開狀態，則您可以在地理處理歷史記錄中找到這些詳細信息。
我們對您所提供的一切信息深表感激。 感謝您為我們改進錯誤代碼文檔所做出的貢獻。

## 評估變量對預測的影響 (空間統計工具) · 匯總

**id**　9500154fba88074056-1

**english**　Evaluate Variable Influence for Predictions

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 空間統計工具箱
- 空間關系建模工具集

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-statistics/evaluate-variable-influence-for-predictions.html#summary

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-statistics/evaluate-variable-influence-for-predictions.html

**text**　評估變量如何影響空間統計模型 (.ssm) 文件中的預測。 該工具創建部分依賴性圖，通過該圖，可以看到每個解釋變量如何影響輸入模型中的預測。
了解有關“評估變量對預測的影響”工作原理的詳細信息

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/spatial-statistics/evaluate-variable-influence-for-predictions.html#summary

## 評估變量對預測的影響 (空間統計工具) · 用法

**id**　9500154fba88074056-2

**english**　Evaluate Variable Influence for Predictions

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 空間統計工具箱
- 空間關系建模工具集

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-statistics/evaluate-variable-influence-for-predictions.html#usage

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-statistics/evaluate-variable-influence-for-predictions.html

**text**　輸入模型文件必須使用基于森林的增強分類與回歸工具進行創建。
您必須提供包含與訓練輸入模型文件所用解釋變量相同的輸入圖徵，并確保解釋變量欄位、距離圖徵和解釋網格相匹配。 該工具會創建一個輸出表，其中包含在預測輸入圖徵時各解釋變量的部分依賴性圖。 這些圖表展示了每個解釋變量如何平均影響預測值，以及變量間關系的形態。
當因變量為分類變量（即分類模型）時，部分依賴性圖將顯示對首個類別進行分類的平均概率。 您可以在圖表屬性窗格中更改圖表顯示的類別。 在資料選項卡中，單擊數值欄位下的選擇按鈕，然后選擇要顯示的類別。 每次僅開啟一個類別，以使圖表能正確顯示部分依賴性圖。
地理處理消息展示了關于輸入模型文件的各種信息，包括模型參數和訓練統計資料。 消息內容取決于模型的創建方式。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/spatial-statistics/evaluate-variable-influence-for-predictions.html#usage

- 標注

**parameter**　explanatory_variable_matching

**interface**　python

**headers**　
- 名稱
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- explanatory_variable_matching [[pred1, train1, cat1], [pred2, train2, cat2],...] （可選）
- 輸入模型的解釋變量列表和輸入圖徵的對應欄位。 對于 Training 列中的每個解釋變量，在 Prediction 列中提供相應的預測欄位。 Categorical 列指定變量是分類變量還是連續變量。 值表列： Prediction—The prediction field of the input features. Training—The explanatory variable of the input model file. Categorical—Specifies whether the variable is categorical or continuous. CATEGORICAL—The explanatory variable is categorical. NUMERIC—The explanatory variable is continuous.
- Value Table

## 評估變量對預測的影響 (空間統計工具) · 代碼示例

**id**　9500154fba88074056-24

**english**　Evaluate Variable Influence for Predictions

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 空間統計工具箱
- 空間關系建模工具集

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-statistics/evaluate-variable-influence-for-predictions.html

**text**　# Create partial dependence plots.  

import arcpy 

# Set the current workspace.
arcpy.env.workspace = r"c:\mydata\mydata.gdb" 

# Run tool.
arcpy.stats.EvaluateVariableInfluence(
    input_model=r"C:\mydata\My_XGBoost_Model.ssm",
    evalutation_type="PDP",
    input_features="Minnesota",
    output_features=None,
    out_table=r"PDP_Chart_Table",
    explanatory_variable_matching="v1_value V1_VALUE false",
    explanatory_distance_matching="citycenter CITYCENTER",
    explanatory_rasters_matching="'LandCover.tif' 'LandCover.tif'"
)

## 評估變量對預測的影響 (空間統計工具) · 工具執行與核對

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 空間統計工具箱
- 空間關系建模工具集

**english**　Evaluate Variable Influence for Predictions

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-statistics/evaluate-variable-influence-for-predictions.html

**id**　9500154fba88074056-workflow

**kind**　工具操作

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-statistics/evaluate-variable-influence-for-predictions.html#parameters

**steps**　
1. 開啟 Analysis > Tools，以英文名稱搜尋 Evaluate Variable Influence for Predictions，核對工具箱及工具摘要。
2. 依工具窗格順序核對下列參數；展開同頁的參數卡可查說明、選項與資料型別。輸入模型文件 [input_model]、評估類型 [evaluation_type]、輸入圖徵 [input_features]、輸出圖徵 （可選） [output_features]、輸出表 （可選） [out_table]、匹配解釋變量 （可選） [explanatory_variable_matching]、匹配距離圖徵 （可選） [explanatory_distance_matching]、匹配解釋網格 （可選） [explanatory_rasters_matching]
3. 依用法、環境與授權段落核對限制。確認輸出位置或就地修改範圍後執行 Run。
4. 在 Geoprocessing History 檢查執行狀態與訊息，再核對結果是否符合使用者指定的範圍與條件。

**provenance**　依官方參數表編排的操作清單

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/spatial-statistics/evaluate-variable-influence-for-predictions.html#parameters

## 180142: 未從 CAD 源文件映射的 DETAILS 圖層導入任何資料。 · 解決方案

**id**　9500439909e45f7cca-2

**english**　180142: No data imported from DETAILS layers mapped from CAD source files.

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 工具錯誤和警告
- 180101-180200

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/180001-190000/tool-errors-and-warnings-180126-180150-180142.html#solution

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/180001-190000/tool-errors-and-warnings-180126-180150-180142.html

**text**　如果出現此異常警告，請驗證“CAD 圖層轉 FC 映射”工作表 DETAILS 列中的值是否正確。

## Generate Land Areas (Maritime Tools) · 匯總

**id**　9504edd7d7ec10bc44-1

**english**　Generate Land Areas

**chapter**　
- 工具參考

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/maritime/generate-land-areas.html#summary

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/maritime/generate-land-areas.html

**text**　Creates land area polygon features by identifying existing land topology features, such as coastline and shoreline construction, and eliminating any polygons over water or other exclusionary features. An area of interest is specified to limit the processing area.
Learn more about how Generate Land Areas works

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/maritime/generate-land-areas.html#summary

## Generate Land Areas (Maritime Tools) · 用法

**id**　9504edd7d7ec10bc44-2

**english**　Generate Land Areas

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- Maritime 工具箱
- 資料管理工具集

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/maritime/generate-land-areas.html#usage

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/maritime/generate-land-areas.html

**text**　Only run this tool with a single usage band. In an enterprise environment, it is recommended that you run this tool on a checkout replica in which the data has been filtered to a single usage band.
For the S-57 schema, the tool automatically populates the NIS_Products field to All and the PLTS_COMP_SCALE field to the current compilation scale value.
For the S-101 schema, the tool automatically populates the minimumDisplayScale and maximumDisplayScale fields to the current minimum and maximum values, respectively.

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**headers**　
- 標注
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- Target Workspace
- The workspace that will contain the land area polygons that are created. The workspace must be a nautical workspace with S-57 or S-101 schema. - Workspace

## Generate Land Areas (Maritime Tools) · 代碼示例

**id**　9504edd7d7ec10bc44-16

**english**　Generate Land Areas

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- Maritime 工具箱
- 資料管理工具集

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/maritime/generate-land-areas.html#derived-output

**text**　# Import arcpy module
import arcpy

in_workspace = r"C:\Data\GLA\enc.gdb"
target_workspace = r"C:\Data\GLA\enc.gdb"
in_extent_polygon = r"C:\Data\GLA\staging.gdb\Extent"
in_configuration_file = r"C:\ArcGISPro\Resources\Maritime\GenerateLandAreasSettings.xml"

arcpy.maritime.GenerateLandAreas(in_workspace, target_workspace, in_extent_polygon, in_configuration_file)

## Generate Land Areas (Maritime Tools) · 工具執行與核對

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- Maritime 工具箱
- 資料管理工具集

**english**　Generate Land Areas

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/maritime/generate-land-areas.html

**id**　9504edd7d7ec10bc44-workflow

**kind**　工具操作

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/maritime/generate-land-areas.html#parameters

**steps**　
1. 開啟 Analysis > Tools，以英文名稱搜尋 Generate Land Areas，核對工具箱及工具摘要。
2. 依工具窗格順序核對下列參數；展開同頁的參數卡可查說明、選項與資料型別。Input Workspace [in_workspace]、Target Workspace [target_workspace]、Extent Polygon Features [in_extent_polygon]、Configuration File （可選） [in_configuration_file]
3. 依用法、環境與授權段落核對限制。確認輸出位置或就地修改範圍後執行 Run。
4. 在 Geoprocessing History 檢查執行狀態與訊息，再核對結果是否符合使用者指定的範圍與條件。

**provenance**　依官方參數表編排的操作清單

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/maritime/generate-land-areas.html#parameters

- arcpy.maritime.GenerateLandAreas(in_workspace, target_workspace, in_extent_polygon, {in_configuration_file})

**text**　依英文版參數表順序列出：in_workspace、target_workspace、in_extent_polygon、in_configuration_file。選填標示、資料型別與英文原說明見 parameters。
